- **grumpy-reviewer**: One grumpy, nitpicky structural reviewer, run as an isolated subagent that treats the code as a junior's submission and judges only maintainability: separation of concerns, service-oriented design, helper extraction, small files, and the rule of 7 (as a grouping nears 7 members, it pushes for sub-groupings). It never learns how the code was produced, only a one-line statement of what it should do, and it reports plainly.
